# MHA SP2 — Supplemental character reconciliation audit

## Responsibility and result

This audit completes the character phase of the authorized three-book reconciliation. It records all **86 baseline readiness subjects**, **25 justified additions to the current table**, all **30 existing dossiers**, the **26 previously identified historical subjects outside the baseline**, additional distributed subjects and the complete reviewed source roster. The [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md) alone assigns current tiers; this audit explains those decisions and the dispositions of people or other entities for which no current row is warranted.

The final current population is **111 subjects: 7 insufficient, 36 emerging, 38 moderate, 17 strong and 13 specialist_ready**. Seventeen baseline subjects move one tier; 69 retain their tiers. Twelve additional subjects enter at moderate and thirteen at emerging. Those 25 are first current normalizations, not invented retroactive promotions. No new dossier is created. The thirty current dossiers receive **90 source-labeled current-support units** and specific qualifications of their existing uncertainty paragraphs. Their original evidence atoms, historical predictions, outcomes, contamination notices and formal provenance tails remain unchanged.

This is character reconciliation, not a claim that every downstream specialist, literary and full-series publication has already converged. The [impact audit](../08%20Audits%20and%20Manifests/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_RECONCILIATION_IMPACT_AUDIT.md) owns overall execution/closeout state; the [aggregate validation](MHA_SP2_AGGREGATE_RECONSTRUCTION_VALIDATION.md) owns retrospective model validation.

## Evidence coverage and counting rule

All **845 original source images** have been visually reviewed and their roster information reconciled: UA 217, UAN 297 and UAG 331. A fresh roster inspection replaced the incomplete recovered UA/UAN notes: root reviewed 119 pages, the source reviewer 303, and the literary reviewer 92; UAG contributes its completed 331-page review and transparent later corrections. Each combined page retains the exact spine locator, original member, image hash, actual printed folio or null, method, named entries, noncharacter scope and uncertainty. The final mechanical comparison matches all 845 members/hashes to both locked maps and current coverage CSVs, with no remaining folio disagreement. This accounts for every page; it does not claim every tiny background face can be named.

The selective ledger contains **1,095 records**, all with explicit dependence fields. These are claim units, not independent behavioral occasions. Its twelve BONUS_FICTION-classified records describe **two five-page narratives**: UA:s0185–s0189 and UAG:s0319–s0323. Reproduced manga, repeated profile statements, two speakers discussing one event and multiple destinations for one record do not multiply the underlying event. No global independent-observation total is inferred mechanically.

Sources and full page coverage: [Ultra Archive audit](../01A%20Supplemental%20Source%20Audits/MHA_SP2_ULTRA_ARCHIVE_PARATEXT_AUDIT.md), [UA coverage](../01A%20Supplemental%20Source%20Audits/MHA_SP2_UA_PAGE_COVERAGE.csv); [Ultra Analysis audit](../01A%20Supplemental%20Source%20Audits/MHA_SP2_ULTRA_ANALYSIS_PARATEXT_AUDIT.md), [UAN coverage](../01A%20Supplemental%20Source%20Audits/MHA_SP2_UAN_PAGE_COVERAGE.csv); [Ultra Age audit](../01A%20Supplemental%20Source%20Audits/MHA_SP2_ULTRA_AGE_PARATEXT_AUDIT.md), [UAG coverage](../01A%20Supplemental%20Source%20Audits/MHA_SP2_UAG_PAGE_COVERAGE.csv).

A name/alias is an occurrence, not an additional person. Exact aliases collapse; role-identified people remain explicit without invented proper names; grouped entries do not become a guessed headcount. Properly distinguished Nomu/constructed actors, the nonhuman principal, a Quirk companion, animals and equipment are reported separately. A role-based identity entry is not proof that every otherwise unnamed figure has been uniquely disambiguated across the entire story. Production contributors, organizations, places, techniques, brands, discarded prototypes and outside-franchise references are excluded from person totals. Poll presence alone supplies no behavior or current reconstruction tier.

The combined census has **348 identity entries**, including two poll labels whose identity/continuity remains unresolved and one hospital-Nomu role whose specific body identity is unresolved. The 111 current subjects plus 237 further entries below exhaust this inventory. Book totals count an identity once per book; cross-book appearances are not additional people. Source labels and reviewer-normalized retrieval labels are retained separately at occurrence level, so a romanization correction is not presented as an official naming variant.

| Scope type | UA | UAN | UAG | Unique across books |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Named diegetic people/actor identities | 104 | 189 | 253 | 259 |
| Role-identified person entries | 8 | 29 | 46 | 68 |
| Other individually identified Nomu | 2 | 4 | 12 | 12 |
| Unresolved hospital-Nomu identity entry | 0 | 0 | 1 | 1 |
| Nonhuman person (Nezu) | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 |
| Quirk companion (Dark Shadow) | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 |
| Named animals (Yui, Mon-chan) | 0 | 1 | 1 | 2 |
| Bird-shaped companion (Pino) | 0 | 0 | 1 | 1 |
| Equipment/AI (Heracles) | 0 | 0 | 1 | 1 |
| Unresolved poll identity labels | 0 | 0 | 2 | 2 |

Kurogiri is already counted as a named current actor identity; the “other Nomu” row does not count him a second time. He and Shirakumo remain distinct model-use subjects, not simple interchangeable aliases. Pino is a visually established companion in the book; no film-only Quirk or biological account is imported. The unnamed Nomu at UAG153 belongs to Crust’s first-war rescue during Decay. Its precise identity among hospital Nomu is unresolved; it is neither merged into USJ nor counted as a proven extra distinct body. Real-world contributors are retained as source credits and scope records, never included in the identity-entry total.

## Identity and source-label adjudications

| Case | Adjudication and retained limit |
|---|---|
| Ingenium | The full label インゲニウム／飯田天晴 identifies Tensei. Bare Ingenium requires page/chronology context; it is not globally assigned to Tenya. Big Inge is Tensei’s later role/name. |
| Haya Yuyu / Habuko Mongoose | 甲矢有弓 is Haya Yuyu. 万偶数羽生子 is Tsuyu’s friend Habuko. Koya Yumi was reviewer romanization error, not another character or official alternate label. |
| Tabe | UAN247–248 からみつ / KARAMITSU and UAG197 そらみつ are a source-attested reading difference for 多部空満. Preserve both witnesses and the existing Tabe Soramitsu retrieval identity; do not force an editorial cause. |
| Contents/poll corrections | Correct current UAG8/9/277 witnesses restore Rin/Muscular and verified labels, including 枡駒子. Goran, the mistaken Sansan/Timothy readings and 榊 for 枡 were reviewer errors. 可児雲隆家 is one person, not a family. Historical checkpoint bytes remain preserved. |
| Kota | The checked bonus says 洸汰くん; 光太 was reviewer transcription error. No claim or stable evidence ID changes. |
| Dark Crystal / Canyon Cannon | UAN39 calls Dark Crystal Tokoyami’s favorite hero, a named diegetic reference with no independent behavioral readiness. UAN283 uses Canyon Cannon as Mt. Lady’s technique. It is excluded from person counts. |
| Relatives and companions | Mika’s brother is role-identified; Yui/結ちゃん/Kawaii-chan is Koda’s rabbit, distinct from Kodai Yui. UAN281 has an NHA reporter and a camera operator, not one merged person. Nabu as 島 is a place. |
| Discarded designs and real guests | Akatani/Mikumo, proposed roles/names, metaleptic studio cameos, real guest artists and contributor hero-style aliases retain production scope. They do not add finalized in-world people or behavioral history. |
| Unresolved figures | UA77 Midoriya, UA93 Iida, UA99 Iida and UA100 Kirishima remain unconfirmed prior identifications, excluded from confirmed person-page occurrences. UA103 Aizawa/Gang Orca were absent reviewer identifications and are explicitly removed. Tiny crowd figures remain groups/uncertainties. |
| Unresolved poll labels | GANRIKI☆NEKO and 暗井指数嫗雄 are retained at their exact poll locators without an established independent identity, continuity or tier. |

The real discrepancies remain distinct from reviewer corrections: UAN’s Yaoyorozu editorial score of 95 conflicts with its reproduced 94 and checked V13:p105; Monoma’s duration statements retain state/epistemic qualifications; Tamaki’s attack captions retain effect-scope tension. Re-Destro’s biological-child wording is more specific than descendant and compatible with the checked V23 account, so no genealogy contradiction is demonstrated. Ratings retain their changing axes and local jokes; no cross-book arithmetic determines readiness.

## Baseline and current roster decisions

The following 86 entries preserve baseline order and identity. “Retain” still means reviewed. A reported practice can narrow a prior gap without supplying varied enacted situations; major characters can gain ordinary or relationship support while retaining strong/specialist_ready. Each new support uses its actual actor and narrative state, not the publication date as a fictional timestamp.

<a id="char-001"></a>
### 001. Midoriya Izuku — specialist_ready retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_MIDORIYA_IZUKU_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Ordinary reception before the traitor disclosure: He treasures letters from Kota and Eri. The relation map calls him close to Aoyama while his own attributed reply admits he cannot read Aoyama’s thinking. This strengthens MI11’s received care without granting him later knowledge of coercion or turning closeness into complete mutual understanding. Adult teaching and public work: The profile and author specify history/hero-history teaching, visits to children’s facilities and schools, and invited speaking alongside armored work. These are continuing roles, not a claim that teaching ceased when the suit arrived; particular classroom decisions remain sparsely dramatized. Later equipment and reciprocal peer speech: The author describes cables and simulated functions; the bonus shows cooperative deployment and an answering joke to Bakugo about their schooldays. The broadcast’s later No.4 is that scene’s chart state, not an intrinsic worth score. The suit does not restore his biological One For All, and the presenter’s conjecture about Bakugo is not Bakugo’s motive.

Coverage decision: Adult encouragement and teaching now have specific ongoing contexts; confident reciprocal banter is also directly shown in the bonus. Neither source licenses a fabricated classroom disciplinary case, unlimited suit capacity or private access to Shigaraki’s feelings. The earlier manga-only heart/life distinction remains intact.

Remaining limits: Adult professional and ordinary-peer coverage improves; precise equipment endurance and unshown private/teaching responses remain open.

<a id="char-002"></a>
### 002. Bakugo Katsuki — specialist_ready retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_BAKUGO_KATSUKI_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Post-finals anger before later guilt and disclosure: The Horikoshi bonus dates itself just after finals. Bakugo’s withheld complaint during All Might’s debrief, Ochako’s unsolicited interpretation, and subsequent eruption are a separate situation from BK04’s later guilt duel and secret disclosure. Her fear interpretation remains hers; his changed expression and anger do not constitute an agreed explanation or reconciliation. Peer reception and family contact: Editorially, rescue, retirement and learning the Quirk truth change his capacity to attend to teammates. His terse parental emails and classmates’ demands for names/better speech retain abrasive ordinary conduct. These statements neither negate change nor make other people’s tolerance consent to every insult. Adult cooperation and particular admiration: The adult profile distinguishes independent work, mixed public reception and better reception in teams. The later bonus enacts cooperative deployment while he needles teacher Midoriya and receives a joke in return. Horikoshi separately confirms an autograph from true-form All Might and thinks Bakugo treasures the armor-remnant memory. Specific admiration and reciprocal banter do not establish uniform politeness; the author’s conjecture retains its qualification.

Coverage decision: The choice of audience, available knowledge and current relational permission should govern reconstruction. A challenge from Ochako immediately after finals is not interchangeable with later accepted aid, guilt disclosure or adult banter. Mountain climbing/spicy-food preferences are earlier profile facts (SUP-UA-024-01), not ready-made behavioral scripts.

Remaining limits: Ordinary speech and state discrimination strengthen; the books do not remove documented injury limits or supply every adult private response.

### 003. Iida Tenya — specialist_ready retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_IIDA_TENYA_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Daily technical friendship: The profile describes daily kicking-technique exchanges with Midoriya; his attributed reply after Hatsume’s sales exploitation considers his own unquestioning trust. These support specific reciprocal learning and self-criticism, not a stereotype of inflexible rule-following. A living, changed family succession: Tenya later heads his brother’s agency, while equipment-supported Tensei returns as his sidekick, called Big Inge. The earlier retirement/rehabilitation state is superseded in this domain. Maternal hero history and care remain part of the family, without inventing a complete household routine. Limits survive improved equipment: Reprinted training preserves imperfect control and a draw; the later catalogue reports extended Turbo duration and the author specifies helmet vision correction. Improved capability does not abolish burden-sharing, bodily constraints or the difference between rivalry and transport duty.

Coverage decision: The adult sibling relation is reciprocal and role-reversed in a particular agency; do not simulate Tensei as permanently absent or as Tenya’s current boss. Daily technique discussion is better supported than an invented independent romantic/private life.

Remaining limits: Family succession, technical companionship and bodily context sharpen the model; unshown private relationships remain open.

<a id="char-004"></a>
### 004. All Might — specialist_ready retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_ALL_MIGHT_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Continuing education and aid office: The current profile makes him U.A.’s vice-principal, with supported independent walking; Eri’s bonus greeting enacts that office. He leads assistance for people caught in villain crime. Neither office nor later mobility restores One For All or cancels physical limitations. Surviving care and individualized mentorship: Inko asks him to live and raise Midoriya; he urges Endeavor to find his own kind of symbol and loses sleep researching powers for his pupil. These dependent manga selections strengthen AM03/AM10’s differentiation of recipients while preserving his vulnerability to self-neglect. Equipment-mediated risk and received rescue: Private funding and skipped safety testing locate the final-war suit’s risk. Heracles can sometimes act autonomously, and others interrupt All Might’s attempted self-destruction. The guide’s bent-fate language is an interpretation of survival, not a tested mechanism or proof self-sacrifice was necessary.

Coverage decision: Use vice-principal and aid coordinator for the later state, with the earlier learner-led teaching policy still available. Declining or accepting risk depends on that dated bodily and relational context. His possible heart-rescue reading remains an interpretation rather than omniscient knowledge.

Remaining limits: The later institutional role and ordinary survivor continuity are better specified; exact private-life and physical limits remain bounded.

<a id="char-005"></a>
### 005. Aizawa Shota — specialist_ready retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_AIZAWA_SHOTA_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Observer limits and care explanations: Early Aizawa thinks Kirishima seems to have few worries; later biography prevents using that impression as complete knowledge. The books connect his severity with losing Shirakumo, while explicitly retaining years of practiced teaching/capture-cloth skill. Grief is a contributing history, not the cause of every decision. Ordinary attention and chosen disclosure: Cats attract his gaze; public appearances are selectively accepted when he considers them necessary. His Aoyama speech refuses to teach lifelong guilt as a way of living. These are distinct from AZ09’s temporary, child-facing Nighteye disclosure boundary; none establishes universal secrecy or unconditional absolution. Adolescent agency in the new bonus: Eri visits with a guitar and gifts. Aizawa recognizes why she wants famous friends to watch her first performance discreetly, calls her stoic and gently corrects playful speech. This is a directly enacted later care context in which he can respect ambition and explain its practical audience constraint.

Coverage decision: For the later Eri, do not mechanically reuse early protection from self-blame as a policy of withholding ordinary decisions. Cat preference can guide attention, but it does not predict adoption or every social choice. Mobility and Erasure remain changed after injury.

Remaining limits: Low-stakes adult/child interaction and recipient-specific care improve; no restored Shirakumo identity or universal teaching omniscience is admitted.

<a id="char-006"></a>
### 006. Uraraka Ochako — specialist_ready retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_URARAKA_OCHAKO_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Material care with independent recipients: Early solitary living and food preferences belong to the student state. Adult Ochako sends money home while her parents leave it untouched. Her care is established; their use of the money and a wholly solved family economy are not. Attraction, speech and personal permission: The early bonus gives unsolicited insight into Bakugo followed by Ochako’s regret at blurting it out. The later school relation map preserves unsettled attraction; Horikoshi directs readers to V42 and imagination when asked about confession. Do not turn interpretive openness into a confirmed off-page relationship transaction. Prevention and including herself: The guide specifies ongoing school counseling tours, while her attributed later voice values laughing together including herself. The manga-dependent grief recap retains self-blame as her speech. These extend the existing distinction between caring for others, receiving care and making room for her own desire.

Coverage decision: A later reconstruction can draw on continuing outreach and an expressed wish to include herself. It cannot infer that every sacrifice stopped, that prevention eliminated its causes, or that her grief establishes sole culpability for Toga’s death.

Remaining limits: Ordinary/material care and expressed self-inclusion sharpen confidence; confession details and dream mechanism remain unconfirmed.

Acceptance has a bounded object: The accepting-father/flame headline coexists with hesitation and watching his father. Inasa’s desired best friendship is not a reciprocal promise. The later recap explicitly separates learning from Endeavor from forgiving what he did. Continuing child-facing and public practice: He repeatedly returns to the Masegaki summer festival to make ice play spaces. The author describes individualized fan attention and qualifies the simple airhead label: he continues taking words straightforwardly. These supply public interaction policy beyond combat, without guaranteeing perfect comprehension. Particular adult friendship and ordinary taste: His attributed report about Bakugo’s nonworking lamp affirms friendship while only guessing at the giver’s reason. Cold-soba preference is an earlier fact. The bonus’s cooperative deployment provides a later shared context, not confirmation of that guessed motive.

Coverage decision: Read a literal response through current context and actual speaker knowledge; avoid making him incapable of irony, every misunderstanding harmless, or all family members reconciled. Children’s play and individualized public attention are supported later uses of his capacities.

Remaining limits: Ordinary language reception and repeated public service improve; family forgiveness and unshown private outcomes remain individually bounded.

Belonging and knowledge are not identical: The general-course classmates plan a farewell party without telling him; his festival contribution strengthens cohesion. <a id="char-010"></a>
### 010. All For One — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_ALL_FOR_ONE_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Named identity without invented conscious memory: His name is Shigaraki Zen. Horikoshi describes choosing the surname from a rumored tanuki story while explicitly saying Zen has no conscious memory of its underlying history. An analyst’s recognized connection cannot become a memory he consciously narrates. An editorial affect theory conflicts with granular evidence: The developmental profile asserts that emotions did not form and tentatively connects this to innate power; another sidebar asks whether Yoichi attachment is love or domination and calls it distorted love. The developmental age/state assertion and later affective vocabulary remain in tension. Retain AF’s observed attachment, rage and control failure. Neither a speculative absence claim nor the love label establishes emotional emptiness, a diagnosis or ethical reciprocity. Capacity and agency limits: Rewind is an administered effect he cannot control, knowingly risked despite disappearance; his final stockpiled attack also injures himself. Large capability does not entail unlimited bodily control or prove every failure planned.

Coverage decision: The current model adds identity provenance and confidence limits to motive interpretation. Possession and affective dependence remain behaviorally grounded even when an official summary proposes a simpler account.

Remaining limits: Source conflict reduces confidence in totalizing psychology; ordinary reciprocal-life variation still prevents specialist readiness.

Home ties are a changing situation: As her parents’ work stabilizes, she and her siblings often video-chat. Frankness does not erase hurt or boundaries: Her attributed descriptions of Mineta’s unwanted touching, refusal of Toga’s assumed intimacy, and hurt over friends’ rescue decisions remain different relational responses. <a id="char-016"></a>
### 016. Hawks — specialist_ready retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_HAWKS_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Audience and knowledge boundaries: Reader-facing name/intelligence knowledge differs from what the public knows. The later compressed Twice account must not replace the manga’s full lethal episode; infiltration, teaching and public explanation have different disclosure rules. Changed body and institutional work: The guide states Fierce Wings was stolen and Hawks subsequently left hero activity to chair the Commission. His stated support for people trying to act rightly is a criterion rather than blanket approval of every act. Nagant is later released and sometimes consulted by him; institutional employment does not restore his Quirk or demonstrate complete repair of the Commission. A private object with a qualified meaning: Horikoshi thinks the childhood Endeavor plush remains somewhere at home because Hawks cannot discard it; he does not display it or buy other merchandise. The author distinguishes this attachment from ordinary fandom. Retain the hedge and do not invent a nightly ritual or uncomplicated parental substitute.

Coverage decision: The object gives ordinary emotional texture while the later consultation relation adds a specific professional channel. No source licenses a single transparent motive behind every cover story or forgiveness for past violence.

Remaining limits: Private attachment and later institutional relationships sharpen; durability of reform and unshown personal routines remain uncertain.

<a id="char-030"></a>
### 030. Kirishima Eijiro — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_KIRISHIMA_EIJIRO_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

A limited early observer is not the whole self: Aizawa’s early impression of few worries is an observer limit, not a contradiction of the later fear/inferiority history. Mina’s warning about overburdening himself and a continuing tactical limitation preserve effort under vulnerability. Disclosure becomes chosen public authorship: Daily color shampoo maintains the school appearance. The author later specifies that he casually told classmates about his reinvention after the second battle and proudly discusses it professionally. The adult calendar’s popularity is reception of that presentation, not proof everyone approves. Kindness and unreciprocated aesthetics: The guide explicitly joins manliness with kindness and explains protection for people he carries. The girls’ boyfriend-room ranking is a framed opinion about his decor, not an objective measure of masculinity or his own shame response.

Coverage decision: The early concealment and later willing public explanation are distinct states. Do not make an adult disclosure threat trigger the same reaction as a threatened school secret, or turn broad popularity into universal interpersonal success.

Remaining limits: Ordinary grooming and later disclosure policy improve; extensive independent adult/private interaction still remains thin.

Source basis: [SUP-UA-037-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-ua-037-01); [SUP-UAG-048-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-048-01); [SUP-UAG-048-02](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-048-02); [SUP-UAG-049-02](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-049-02); [SUP-UAG-301-08](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-301-08); [SUP-UAN-035-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-035-01); [SUP-UAN-035-02](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-035-02).

<a id="char-033"></a>
### 033. Mineta Minoru — emerging retained

No standalone dossier. Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Intelligence, cooperative tactics, fear-borne courage and sincere attachment coexist with repeated sexual-boundary misconduct. He makes a major arrest while Mt. Lady’s sidekick before becoming independent; later criticism follows DMs to female fans. The messages’ content, recipients’ ages/consent and his response are unspecified. Editorial softening remains a rhetorical question.

Coverage decision: Adult role and reception add context, but no new observed criticism-response regime closes moderate; moral approval is not a readiness criterion.

Remaining limits: Independent ordinary relationships and responses to criticism/repair remain thin. Neither competence nor prosocial risk establishes recognition of harm, changed boundaries or rehabilitation.

### 034. Sero Hanta — emerging → moderate

A specific adult criticism response—worry that shaving a disliked beard would look affected—joins casual Bakugo/Shoto contact, transferable technique explanation and rejection of tragedy as a prerequisite for strength. The reason for Bondo event pairings stays unknown.

<a id="char-035"></a>
### 035. Jiro Kyoka — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_JIRO_KYOKA_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Received care and reciprocal taste: Jiro credits Koda and apologizes for pressing his insect fear; Momo introduces classical music while she introduces metal in the reciprocal profile. Later praise can be redirected to Kaminari’s learning. Expertise is compatible with gratitude and being a learner. Continuing chosen music and mentoring: Adult music continues more actively, and the author says she apparently teaches Eri guitar/bass when schedules permit. The bonus reports clearing time for Eri’s first performance. This specifies a continuing chosen relationship, not mandatory availability or a complete music-career schedule. Repair is not original bodily restoration: The profile explicitly says her repaired left ear is not back to its original state. The reprinted final-war work remains cooperation under fear and injury. Do not project a full functional recovery into every later action or turn an effect on AFO into raw superiority.

Coverage decision: For an invited musical project, role choice, schedule and the learner’s needs matter. Neighboring offices are author-confirmed, but proximity still does not establish romance or undo her manga boundary-setting.

Remaining limits: Independent musical and mentoring contexts materially improve; private adult relationship variability and precise medical limits remain open.

<a id="char-037"></a>
### 037. Shoji Mezo — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_SHOJI_MEZO_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Prejudice and masking retain exact scope: The regional prejudice/mask profile sits uneasily beside the glossary’s broadly prejudice-free modernity wording. His manga speech also gives not wanting to be seen as a revenger as a masking reason. Neither a generic glossary nor a crying-girl explanation erases the more granular history and self-presentation. Ordinary peer comfort and continued care: Comfortable taciturn company with Koda, adult off-duty Japanese clothing and the attributed laugh at Koda’s hair observation add ordinary texture. They do not make him silent in every setting or establish a broad private adult social network. Received thanks has an explicitly external disclosure: Horikoshi says the girl recognized him at the Sports Festival but delayed contact to avoid bringing painful history back; after the second war she sent thanks and a smiling photo. Her reasons are her side of the account. The author says he forgot to draw the planned interstitial, and Shoji’s detailed response remains unshown.

Coverage decision: The received letter strengthens the continuity of a particular rescue relationship without inventing a reunion scene or reaction. His broader public work remains high confidence; use conversational economy as conditional, not total emotional opacity.

Remaining limits: Low-stakes peer interaction and received care improve; private adult relationship variation remains limited and the V37 author drawing stays excluded from historical inputs.

<a id="char-039"></a>
### 039. Spinner / Iguchi Shuichi — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_SPINNER_IGUCHI_SHUICHI_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

A corrected ability and differentiated belonging: UA’s sword-as-Quirk conjecture gives way to wall adhesion; official later accounts distinguish Stain identification from Tomura-specific attachment and name Spinner as the game partner. Do not use an early compact fanatic label to erase changed belonging. Publication goes beyond the manga’s intended book: The guide and author state the memoir was published, provoked heavy criticism and gained some historical recognition; Compress is shown receiving it positively. Those are distinct readers’ responses. Publication is no longer merely an intention, but broad acceptance and objective historical completeness remain unestablished. Friend-directed action can have unwanted consequences: Trying to revive Tomura also strengthens AFO’s will, while added bodily powers cause pain and impairment. Intention, achieved effect and usable cognition must remain separate; fidelity does not make him an infallible interpreter of his friend.

Coverage decision: A later memorial/reader situation can now assume publication and mixed reception. It still cannot assume how Spinner privately responds to each critic or recover unrecorded adult relationships outside the League.

Remaining limits: Authorship now has an explicitly reported publication/reception context; private and non-League relationship breadth remains limited.

<a id="char-040"></a>
### 040. Aoyama Yuga — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_AOYAMA_YUGA_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Closeness before shared knowledge: The school-era profile links Aoyama’s care to perceived similar Quirk struggles, while Midoriya’s attributed response says he cannot read Aoyama’s thoughts. UAG’s retrospective identification of shared former quirklessness does not grant Midoriya that knowledge during the cheese exchange. French origin remains Aoyama’s own claim, not verified nationality. Action with fear and chosen accountability: Protection promised by Aizawa and concern for friends/family support participation in the lure while Aoyama explicitly remains afraid. His later voluntary withdrawal and renewed hero goal are distinct choices after disclosure. Neither cooperation nor classmates’ acceptance means the fear disappeared or accountability was imposed in the exact form he chose. Later licensed work in a specific partnership: Following judicial process, he eventually obtains a hero license and works as Hagakure’s sidekick. The guide describes laughing with everyone as equals; the author dates her later visibility bug to work after recruiting him. This establishes professional reintegration and a particular partnership, not romantic reciprocity, every legal detail or a broadly sampled independent adult personality.

Coverage decision: An earlier Aoyama must retain coercion, concealment and his interlocutor’s limited knowledge. The later model can assume a licensed role and the Hagakure working relation without reducing all care to deception or treating reintegration as evidence that no consequences remain.

Remaining limits: Post-disclosure agency and later professional belonging are more concrete; varied independent adult conflicts, legal particulars and private relationships remain limited.

<a id="char-042"></a>
### 042. Himiko Toga — specialist_ready retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_HIMIKO_TOGA_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Clothing combines pleasure and evasion: Horikoshi says she genuinely likes sailor uniforms and considers herself cute in them; favored casual clothes are bought and discarded once she is satisfied, also confusing pursuit. This is an author-described ordinary practice with dual purposes, not a diagnosis or an inferred motive from costume alone. Attribution does not supply unknown acquisition: UAN offers an editorial reason for keeping Camie alive, while UAG still leaves the blood-acquisition route unknown. Keep the explanatory claim as attributed and the method open; Camie’s behavior while impersonated is not real Camie evidence. Chosen terminal rescue does not erase prior harms: The guide retains childhood suppression, destructive action and affection-conditioned power alongside the lethal blood gift to Ochako. It describes dying while living according to her likes. Specific rescue is not a general consent repair or a redeemed adult future.

Coverage decision: Clothing choice and concealment now have concrete support, but preference is not universal behavioral permission. Distinguish appearance copying, affection-conditioned Quirk use, the original actor and duplicates; grief/dream reception after death is not autonomous speech.

Remaining limits: Ordinary self-presentation improves; harm/consent distinctions and unknown mechanisms remain.

<a id="char-043"></a>
### 043. Dabi / Todoroki Toya — specialist_ready retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_DABI_TODOROKI_TOYA_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Concealment and inherited ambition: The guide says his father’s aspiration became his own childhood dream, then traces rejection into hatred; dyed hair conceals him from family until disclosure. These are dated conditions, not knowledge every observer possessed earlier. Instrumental kindness is a question to test: The sidebar asks whether even kindness to Toga is another use of available means; sharing Twice’s blood and publicizing his death serve destructive plans. Preserve the evidence and interrogative framing rather than declaring all possible affection unreal. Medical trajectory does not expand remorse: The later profile describes family time in a body slowly approaching death. It does not date death, overturn the V42 altar implication, establish full rehabilitation or broaden DY07’s specific apology into forgiveness by every family member.

Coverage decision: Concealed identity, public performance, bodily decline and the narrow sibling apology are different reconstruction states. A family visit cannot be treated as consent to every earlier harm or evidence of an open healthy future.

Remaining limits: Concealment and motive framing sharpen; exact death timing and broader remorse remain unshown.

### 044. Mr. Compress / Sako Atsuhiro — moderate retained

No standalone dossier. Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Imprisonment remains current; smiling reception of Spinner’s published book adds a particular later reader response. Author-described disappearing skills and regret over underused magician movement are production/capacity context, not new enacted tricks.

Coverage decision: Postwar reception adds a state-specific context; most personality evidence repeats the terminal performance/loyalty domain.

Remaining limits: Non-League intimacy, ordinary independent choices and varied private responses still gate strong. An ancestry slogan is not innate villain psychology.

<a id="char-050"></a>
### 050. Togata Mirio — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_TOGATA_MIRIO_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Humor has differentiated recipients: Some elderly recipients question whether he is a comedian, while the author says the adult peach-harvesting variation pleases children. Persistent humor is neither universal success nor evidence grief vanished; choose audience and current stakes before predicting reception. Mentorship is not merely a succession instrument: The guide says Mirio’s existence became Nighteye’s pride beyond the original One For All succession plan. This is retrospective relational framing, not a new omniscient account of every earlier evaluation. An agency sequence, then a separate chosen invitation: After graduation he first joins the inherited Nighteye agency as a sidekick, then later takes it over. Eri includes him among beloved heroes whose fame she does not want to use for her first performance. Being admired does not entitle him to determine her stage or audience.

Coverage decision: Distinguish practiced encouragement from an expectation that every joke lands. Current agency leadership is established; specific management decisions and reactions to Eri’s stated boundary are not directly enacted.

Remaining limits: Adult recipient variation and professional continuity improve; wider private/conflict responses remain the specialist gap.

Source basis: [SUP-UAG-088-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-088-01); [SUP-UAG-089-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-089-01); [SUP-UAG-101-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-101-01); [SUP-UAG-138-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-138-01); [SUP-UAG-303-04](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-303-04); [SUP-UAG-320-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-320-01).

<a id="char-051"></a>
### 051. Sir Nighteye — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_SIR_NIGHTEYE_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Humor, care and workplace coercion coexist: Merchandise, fandom and valuing humor are specific ordinary practices. The tickling-machine punishment remains a coercive gag with no established employee consent. A warm retrospective portrayal does not make it ordinary kindness or require imitation in a novel workplace. A particular deathbed disclosure: The guide develops pride in Mirio beyond a succession project and preserves his statement to All Might that he bears no resentment. This final relation-specific disclosure does not mean he never struggled with regret or felt no earlier hurt. An uncertain sacrificial intention stays uncertain: UAN asks whether he took the fatal blow to alter the future; the question is not established motive. His later appearance through All Might’s remembered prediction is not an independently living return or proof every future can be changed.

Coverage decision: A chronology-specific Nighteye can combine exacting standards, personal attachments and imperfect humor. Do not import deathbed openness into an earlier closed forecast or turn posthumous agency succession into his own later decisions.

Remaining limits: Ordinary and relationship interpretation sharpens; independent nonprofessional life remains thin and no postmortem actor state is created.

<a id="char-052"></a>
### 052. Chisaki / Overhaul — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_CHISAKI_OVERHAUL_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Quirk modification is asserted but operationally unspecified: UAN says the severed arms were treated and the Quirk modified; it supplies no restored hands, activation method or later executed use. UAG’s separate degraded-copy origin explanation does not fill those omissions. Keep the claim visible and attributed rather than assuming either full recovery or total factor erasure. Selective material concern: The book states he pays the comatose boss’s hospital costs while pursuing a plan that exploits Eri. The payment mechanism is unknown. Care for one recipient cannot be generalized into humane treatment of everyone he instrumentalizes. Reunion is not completed accountability: He is imprisoned again; medical support restores the boss’s consciousness, and the boss demands lifelong Eri-directed apology. The editorial reconciliation label concerns that encounter. Eri is separated from the Hassaikai and cared for elsewhere, not returned as proof of his reform.

Coverage decision: A later hypothetical must distinguish boss-directed regret from unshown response to Eri’s needs. No functional-Quirk scenario should assume the missing activation detail; confidence in a total incapacity claim is correspondingly restricted.

Remaining limits: Specific concern and institutional aftermath sharpen; completed reform, broad reciprocity and restored function remain unestablished.

Source basis: [SUP-UAG-150-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-150-01); [SUP-UAG-196-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-196-01); [SUP-UAG-197-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-197-01); [SUP-UAG-216-02](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-216-02); [SUP-UAN-243-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-243-01); [SUP-UAN-243-02](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-243-02); [SUP-UAN-261-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-261-01).

<a id="char-053"></a>
### 053. Lady Nagant / Tsutsumi Kaina — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_LADY_NAGANT_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Refusal and release belong to different times: Her initial decision to wait reflects doubt about society’s response to the heroes. The later profile explicitly states release, hobbies and occasional Hawks consultation. The V42 custodial snapshot is not the permanent current state, and no release date, hobby list or full rehabilitation process is supplied. Caring essence is editorial framing: The guide sees continuity in care and inability to ignore someone in front of her; Rock Lock explicitly takes responsibility for allowing her dangerous aid. Preserve her choice and bodily risk. A benevolent essence label does not erase coerced or authored killing, and another’s permission is not full recovery. Concealed institutional violence: The killing of the earlier Commission president was concealed and Tartarus constrained information. Reader knowledge of that account should not become universal public knowledge or be transferred to the later Commission without distinction.

Coverage decision: Later ordinary-life reconstruction may assume freedom and unspecified hobbies, not a continuing cell or a particular imagined pastime. Consultation with Hawks supports a professional relation while broader private response remains sparse.

Remaining limits: This materially revises the current custody/ordinary-life boundary; diverse civilian interactions and precise recovery limits remain missing.

<a id="char-057"></a>
### 057. Amajiki Tamaki — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_AMAJIKI_TAMAKI_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Encouragement can also produce pressure: Fat Gum’s praise and Kirishima’s intensity can burden or frighten him even when intended warmly. Friends can help him act without removing anxiety. Use the recipient’s reaction rather than treating all received support as immediately soothing. Food becomes curiosity rather than only equipment: Horikoshi says childhood fear of foods was overtaken by pleasure in their manifestation and that he now eats with curiosity. This supplies a material ordinary-life change; it does not identify every preferred food, allergy, restaurant behavior or the end of all anxiety. Chosen employment and qualified capability: He remains Fat Gum’s sidekick to repay him; a hypothetical rank beyond Mirio is rumor. The great combined attack is called ineffective in the profile but arm-blowing in the move caption: record that tension and do not make an unlimited-Manifest slogan a guaranteed tactical result.

Coverage decision: Curiosity around food is now better supported than universal instrumental eating. Gratitude-based employment is a choice, not inability to lead, while independent adult team-management response still lacks samples.

Remaining limits: A clear ordinary domain improves; catastrophic-power summaries do not resolve broad adult social/failure variation.

<a id="char-060"></a>
### 060. Eri — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_ERI_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Ongoing life and a later care arrangement: She becomes a high-school music-club member after singing with guitar in middle school. Mandalay volunteers to receive her after talks with the awakened boss, with Pussycats care shared. No legal-adoption date, exclusive residence or completed career is given. Affection without using borrowed celebrity: The Horikoshi bonus directly shows her visiting familiar teachers with guitar/gifts and saying she does not want her first street performance to ride Deku’s or Lemillion’s fame. Loving them and wanting discreet attendance coexist. This is new adolescent self-presentation, not merely gratitude repaying rescue. Inspired independent effort: Watching Midoriya at work prompts her own resolve while she remains with her guitar. Jiro’s teaching is schedule-dependent. Her musical action belongs to her own project; it is not renewed sacrifice of her horn, guaranteed trauma resolution or a medical prognosis.

Coverage decision: Do not mechanically simulate the adolescent as the silent self-blaming child. Familiar affection, playful speech and a chosen performance boundary are observed; the public debut is still future to the scene and cannot be claimed completed.

Remaining limits: Adolescent ordinary speech and motive differentiation improve substantially; peer conflict, sustained independent life and exhaustive Quirk prognosis still limit specialist readiness.

<a id="char-067"></a>
### 067. Gentle Criminal — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_GENTLE_CRIMINAL_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Tea is a practiced, imperfect performance: Tea enthusiasm includes rapid brand talk even to an opponent and the gag of spilling during elegant drinking. This gives a particular low-stakes conversational repertoire; gentlemanly presentation is not flawless composure or moral innocence. A changed livelihood and authority relation: Gentle and La Brava found GeL Inc.; Aiba is president and Gentle is her secretary. La Brava leads the engineer coalition. Do not reverse their offices or assume the former criminal-performance hierarchy persists in the company. Recognition does not expunge harm: The guide calls the final-war rescue heroic while explicitly saying earlier wrongdoing does not disappear. La Brava’s amplification and his elastic support are distinct contributions. Later success is not retroactive proof that his earlier dangerous performances were harmless.

Coverage decision: Current business support and tea conversation are more specific than the manga-only endpoint. A new employee or professional disagreement response is still underdetermined; cooperation with La Brava does not supply every nonpartner relationship.

Remaining limits: Ordinary performance and reintegration gain concrete form; wider independent relationship responses still gate specialist readiness.

<a id="char-069"></a>
### 069. Tokoyami Fumikage — strong retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_TOKOYAMI_FUMIKAGE_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Ordinary taste and imperfect skill: Dark Crystal inspires his choker; his attributed guitar reply admits giving up at the F chord. Dramatic names express an aesthetic, not universal eloquence, hostility or competence in every cool-looking activity. Childhood accommodation to a companion: Horikoshi supplies the recurrent bedtime exchange: strong light prevents rampage, Tokoyami apologizes for it, and Dark Shadow accepts it. This is author-described backstory behind a brief V38 image, not a newly discovered extensive manga scene. It materially adds ordinary protective negotiation with the companion. Adult trust and conditional control: He sometimes assists licensing examinations at Hawks’ request. The catalogue reports trained control in complete darkness, while the general entry still ties darkness to greater difficulty. Treat acquired control as situated capability; neither statement establishes immunity to all affective/stress conditions.

Coverage decision: Reciprocal accommodation is now better supported than a model of simply suppressing a tool. Keep Hawks-facing trust, dark-aesthetic peer talk and actual light/power constraints distinct. Named Dark Shadow is not another guise of Tokoyami.

Remaining limits: Ordinary companion care and adult instruction broaden the model; independent private-human relationships remain a specialist gap.

Source basis: [SUP-UA-145-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-ua-145-01); [SUP-UAN-206-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-206-01); [SUP-UAG-194-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-194-01).

<a id="char-079"></a>
### 079. Twice / Bubaigawara Jin — specialist_ready retained

[Current dossier](MHA_SP2_TWICE_BUBAIGAWARA_JIN_CHARACTER_RECONSTRUCTION_MODEL.md) Current authority: [readiness index](MHA_SP2_CHARACTER_MODEL_READINESS_INDEX.md).

Apparatus and felt bodily security: His own stated need for the whole body to feel enclosed is paired with mask and high-grade durable clothing. Unlocking self-copying after a specific recognition does not prove all fear or vulnerability cured. Material comfort is more specific than a generic trauma label. Different companions, different attachment: The book separates Toga-directed comradeship, executive recognition and repeated operational company, then centers happiness of companions who accepted his loneliness. Relationship shorthand is not equal intimacy with every League member. Contradictory speech and terminal actor: The gag asks whether contrary statements are both sincere; this is not clinical diagnosis. The helper reaching Toga is a duplicate after the original’s death, and Toga’s later proliferation is her action using his blood. Preserve those actor/death distinctions.

Coverage decision: A novel earlier situation involving exposure or suit damage should consider bodily security, not only abstract identity belief. His final care remains particular; there is no postmortem adult Twice model.

Remaining limits: Embodied reassurance and relational discrimination improve, without claiming generalized psychological cure.

Source basis: [SUP-UAG-181-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-181-01); [SUP-UAG-182-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-182-01); [SUP-UAG-183-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-183-01); [SUP-UAG-183-02](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-183-02); [SUP-UAG-258-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uag-258-01); [SUP-UAN-231-01](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-231-01); [SUP-UAN-231-02](../07%20Evidence%20and%20Indexes/MHA_SP2_SUPPLEMENTAL_EVIDENCE_RECONCILIATION_LEDGER.md#sup-uan-231-02).

These 25 subjects were absent from the normalized top table. Their exact source-and-gate decisions now justify current rows while leaving older local states untouched. Existing distributed references are retained where located. “Additional book subject” means newly enrolled through this reconciliation inventory; it does not assert that the person originated in a guidebook or had never been mentioned anywhere in the repository. No new strong/specialist_ready tier or dossier is assigned.

## Further source subjects and dispositions

The remaining **237 entries** receive explicit no-current-row dispositions below. These are not hidden competing tier assignments. Absence of a current row assigns no new `insufficient` tier. Source record association is a retrieval route: a multi-subject record is not equally strong behavioral evidence for every named person. The complete source locators preserve subjects omitted from the selective ledger, including named poll, profile, glossary, gallery and cameo entries. Existing distributed references are linked where found; lookup presence alone is not a fresh full assessment of an entire old file.

## Noncharacter, collective and qualified retrieval records

Every structured entity-bearing field is accounted for, including string poll entries, organizations, real contributors, named/normalized rosters, excluded mentions and prototype fields. Original notes and later identification corrections remain attached to the page record. The categories below report distinct **source/reviewer labels**, not unique people; spelling, role or contextual variants may produce several labels for one contributor or organization. None inflates the 348 identity entries.

## Preservation, validation and discovery

The complete pre-edit files were retained. Thirty dossier edits are limited to the current source-boundary field, a source-specific qualification after the existing OPEN uncertainty paragraph, and a new supplemental section immediately before the formal probe. Reversing those declared additions/source-boundary changes reproduces every original dossier byte. The thirty formal-probe/provenance tails remain byte-identical. The readiness index’s historical tail beginning at “V02 readiness changes” also remains byte-identical; no prior tier transaction, prediction, outcome, scoring or contamination field is rewritten.

The current reconciliation is **retrospective, non-blind and unscored**. No new hypothetical scenario or guidebook consistency check is represented as independent predictive validation. The [aggregate validation](MHA_SP2_AGGREGATE_RECONSTRUCTION_VALIDATION.md) retains the original thirty comparisons and owns the separate source-reconciliation assessment. Profile/author information may expand the usable current model without retrospectively making a frozen prediction better.

The global registry’s 30 MHA subject records and existing discovery evidence paths are preserved. New lower-tier current entries and further candidates are made discoverable by this audit and the current readiness table; global registry enrollment remains the authorized curator’s responsibility under the maintained discovery policy. No registry upsert, generated global character index edit or automatic dossier creation occurs in this phase.
